        queueManager.currentTrack?.let { applyEffectivePreamp(it) } ?: audioEngine.setPreamp(gainDb)
    }

    fun getPreamp(): Float = preferencesManager.getPreamp()

    fun applyEffectivePreamp(track: Track) {
        val userPreamp = preferencesManager.getPreamp()
        val rgMode = preferencesManager.getReplayGainMode()
        val rgGain = when (rgMode) {
            1 -> { // Track Gain
                if (track.replayGainTrackGain != 0.0f) {
                    track.replayGainTrackGain + preferencesManager.getReplayGainPreampWithRg()
                } else {
                    preferencesManager.getReplayGainPreampWithoutRg()
                }
            }
            2 -> { // Album Gain
                if (track.replayGainAlbumGain != 0.0f) {
                    track.replayGainAlbumGain + preferencesManager.getReplayGainPreampWithRg()
                } else {
                    preferencesManager.getReplayGainPreampWithoutRg()
                }
            }
            else -> 0.0f
        }
        val effective = (userPreamp + rgGain).coerceIn(-12.0f, 12.0f)
        audioEngine.setPreamp(effective)
    }

    fun setReplayGainMode(mode: Int) {
        preferencesManager.saveReplayGainMode(mode)
        queueManager.currentTrack?.let { applyEffectivePreamp(it) }
    }

    fun getReplayGainMode(): Int = preferencesManager.getReplayGainMode()

    fun setBass(gainDb: Float) {
        audioEngine.setBass(gainDb)
        preferencesManager.saveBass(gainDb)
    }

    fun getBass(): Float = audioEngine.getBass()

    fun setTreble(gainDb: Float) {
        audioEngine.setTreble(gainDb)
        preferencesManager.saveTreble(gainDb)
    }

    fun getTreble(): Float = audioEngine.getTreble()

    fun setToneEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        audioEngine.setToneEnabled(enabled)
        preferencesManager.saveToneEnabled(enabled)
    }

    fun isToneEnabled(): Boolean = audioEngine.isToneEnabled()

    fun setBalance(balance: Float) {
        audioEngine.setBalance(balance)
        preferencesManager.saveBalance(balance)
    }

    fun getBalance(): Float = audioEngine.getBalance()

    fun setMono(mono: Boolean) {
        audioEngine.setMono(mono)
        preferencesManager.saveMono(mono)
    }

    fun isMono(): Boolean = audioEngine.isMono()

    // --- Spatial Audio DSP ---

    fun setSpatialMode(mode: Int) {
        audioEngine.setSpatialMode(mode)
        preferencesManager.spatialMode = mode
    }

    fun getSpatialMode(): Int = preferencesManager.spatialMode

    fun setSpatialPreset(preset: Int, intensity: Float) {
        audioEngine.setSpatialPreset(preset, intensity)
        preferencesManager.spatialPreset = preset
        preferencesManager.spatialIntensity = intensity
    }

    fun getSpatialPreset(): Int = preferencesManager.spatialPreset
    fun getSpatialIntensity(): Float = preferencesManager.spatialIntensity

    fun setSpatialProParams(
        subCutoffHz: Float,
        midGainDb: Float,
        sideWidth: Float,
        surroundAmbience: Float,
        speakerAngle: Float,
        elevation: Float = 0f
    ) {
        audioEngine.setSpatialProParams(subCutoffHz, midGainDb, sideWidth, surroundAmbience, speakerAngle, elevation)
        preferencesManager.spatialSubCutoff = subCutoffHz
        preferencesManager.spatialMidGain = midGainDb
        preferencesManager.spatialSideWidth = sideWidth
        preferencesManager.spatialSurround = surroundAmbience
        preferencesManager.spatialAngle = speakerAngle
        preferencesManager.spatialElevation = elevation
    }

    fun setCrossfadeDurationMs(ms: Int) {
        preferencesManager.saveCrossfadeDurationMs(ms)
    }

    fun getCrossfadeDurationMs(): Int = preferencesManager.getCrossfadeDurationMs()

    private fun startProgressUpdates() {
        handler.removeCallbacks(progressRunnable)
        handler.post(progressRunnable)
    }

    private fun notifyTrack(track: Track?) {
        listeners.forEach { it.onTrackChanged(track) }
    }

    private fun notifyState(isPlaying: Boolean) {
        listeners.forEach { it.onPlaybackStateChanged(isPlaying) }
    }

    private fun notifyProgress(currentMs: Long, durationMs: Long) {
        listeners.forEach { it.onProgressUpdate(currentMs, durationMs) }
    }

    private fun notifyQueue() {
        val tracks = queueManager.getTracks()
        val index = queueManager.currentQueueIndex
        listeners.forEach { it.onQueueUpdated(tracks, index) }
    }

    private fun notifyError(trackId: Long, trackTitle: String) {
        handler.post {
            listeners.forEach { it.onPlaybackError(trackId, trackTitle) }
        }
    }

    private fun saveCurrentState() {
        val ids = queueManager.getTracks().map { it.id }
        preferencesManager.saveQueue(ids, queueManager.currentQueueIndex, audioEngine.getPositionMs())
    }

    // --- MediaSession setup ---

    private var mediaButtonClickCount = 0
    private val mediaButtonHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    private val mediaButtonRunnable = Runnable {
        when (mediaButtonClickCount) {
            1 -> togglePlayPause()
            2 -> playNext()
            3 -> playPrevious()
        }
        mediaButtonClickCount = 0
    }

    private fun initMediaSession() {
        mediaSession = MediaSession(this, "PowerampCleanSession").apply {
            setCallback(object : MediaSession.Callback() {
                override fun onPlay() = this@PlaybackService.play()
                override fun onPause() = this@PlaybackService.pause()
                override fun onSkipToNext() = this@PlaybackService.playNext()
                override fun onSkipToPrevious() = this@PlaybackService.playPrevious()
                override fun onSeekTo(pos: Long) = this@PlaybackService.seekTo(pos)
                override fun onStop() = this@PlaybackService.stop()

                override fun onMediaButtonEvent(mediaButtonIntent: Intent): Boolean {
                    val event = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
                        mediaButtonIntent.getParcelableExtra(Intent.EXTRA_KEY_EVENT, KeyEvent::class.java)
                    } else {
                        @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                        mediaButtonIntent.getParcelableExtra(Intent.EXTRA_KEY_EVENT)
                    }

                    if (event != null && event.action == KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) {
                        when (event.keyCode) {
                            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_HEADSETHOOK,
                            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MEDIA_PLAY_PAUSE -> {
                                mediaButtonClickCount++
                                mediaButtonHandler.removeCallbacks(mediaButtonRunnable)
                                if (mediaButtonClickCount >= 3) {
                                    mediaButtonRunnable.run()
                                } else {
                                    mediaButtonHandler.postDelayed(mediaButtonRunnable, 320)
                                }
                                return true
                            }
                            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MEDIA_NEXT -> {
                                playNext()
                                return true
                            }
                            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MEDIA_PREVIOUS -> {
                                playPrevious()
                                return true
                            }
                            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MEDIA_PLAY -> {
                                play()
                                return true
                            }
                            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MEDIA_PAUSE -> {
                                pause()
                                return true
                            }
                            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_MEDIA_STOP -> {
                                stop()
                                return true
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    return super.onMediaButtonEvent(mediaButtonIntent)
                }
            })
            setFlags(
                MediaSession.FLAG_HANDLES_MEDIA_BUTTONS or
                MediaSession.FLAG_HANDLES_TRANSPORT_CONTROLS
            )
            isActive = true
        }
    }

    private fun updateMediaSessionMetadata(track: Track) {
        val baseMeta = MediaMetadata.Builder()
            .putString(MediaMetadata.METADATA_KEY_TITLE, track.title)
            .putString(MediaMetadata.METADATA_KEY_ARTIST, track.artist)
            .putString(MediaMetadata.METADATA_KEY_ALBUM, track.album)
            .putLong(MediaMetadata.METADATA_KEY_DURATION, track.durationMs)
            .build()
        mediaSession.setMetadata(baseMeta)

        // Asynchronously load artwork for Android 13/14/15/16 Quick Settings control center
        CoverArtLoader.loadCover(this, track, 512) { bitmap ->
            if (bitmap != null && queueManager.currentTrack?.id == track.id) {
                currentCoverBitmap = bitmap
                try {
                    val palette = Palette.from(bitmap).generate()
                    currentAccentColor = palette.getVibrantColor(palette.getDominantColor(0xFFF5881F.toInt()))
                } catch (_: Exception) {}
                val fullMeta = MediaMetadata.Builder()
                    .putString(MediaMetadata.METADATA_KEY_TITLE, track.title)
                    .putString(MediaMetadata.METADATA_KEY_ARTIST, track.artist)
                    .putString(MediaMetadata.METADATA_KEY_ALBUM, track.album)
                    .putLong(MediaMetadata.METADATA_KEY_DURATION, track.durationMs)
                    .putBitmap(MediaMetadata.METADATA_KEY_ALBUM_ART, bitmap)
                    .putBitmap(MediaMetadata.METADATA_KEY_ART, bitmap)
                    .build()
                mediaSession.setMetadata(fullMeta)
                showNotification()
            }
        }
    }

    private fun updateMediaSessionState(state: Int, positionMs: Long) {
        val actions = PlaybackState.ACTION_PLAY or
                PlaybackState.ACTION_PAUSE or
                PlaybackState.ACTION_PLAY_PAUSE or
                PlaybackState.ACTION_SKIP_TO_NEXT or
                PlaybackState.ACTION_SKIP_TO_PREVIOUS or
                PlaybackState.ACTION_SEEK_TO or
                PlaybackState.ACTION_STOP

        val playbackState = PlaybackState.Builder()
            .setActions(actions)
            .setState(state, positionMs, 1.0f)
            .build()

        mediaSession.setPlaybackState(playbackState)
    }

    // --- Audio Focus Handling ---

    private fun requestAudioFocus(): Boolean {
        if (hasAudioFocus) return true

        val result = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            val attr = AudioAttributes.Builder()
                .setUsage(AudioAttributes.USAGE_MEDIA)
                .setContentType(AudioAttributes.CONTENT_TYPE_MUSIC)
                .build()

            val request = AudioFocusRequest.Builder(AudioManager.AUDIOFOCUS_GAIN)
                .setAudioAttributes(attr)
                .setAcceptsDelayedFocusGain(true)
                .setOnAudioFocusChangeListener(this, handler)
                .build()

            audioFocusRequest = request
            audioManager.requestAudioFocus(request)
        } else {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            audioManager.requestAudioFocus(this, AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC, AudioManager.AUDIOFOCUS_GAIN)
        }

        hasAudioFocus = (result == AudioManager.AUDIOFOCUS_REQUEST_GRANTED)
        return hasAudioFocus
    }

    private fun abandonAudioFocus() {
        if (!hasAudioFocus) return

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            audioFocusRequest?.let { audioManager.abandonAudioFocusRequest(it) }
        } else {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            audioManager.abandonAudioFocus(this)
        }
        hasAudioFocus = false
    }

    override fun onAudioFocusChange(focusChange: Int) {
        when (focusChange) {
            AudioManager.AUDIOFOCUS_LOSS -> {
                hasAudioFocus = false
                pause()
            }
            AudioManager.AUDIOFOCUS_LOSS_TRANSIENT -> {
                hasAudioFocus = false
                if (audioEngine.isPlaying()) {
                    pausedByTransientLoss = true
                    audioEngine.pause()
                    wasPlaying = false
                    handler.removeCallbacks(progressRunnable)
                    notifyState(false)
                    showNotification()
                }
            }
            AudioManager.AUDIOFOCUS_LOSS_TRANSIENT_CAN_DUCK -> {
                audioEngine.setVolume(0.2f)
            }
            AudioManager.AUDIOFOCUS_GAIN -> {
                hasAudioFocus = true
                audioEngine.setVolume(1.0f)
                if (pausedByTransientLoss) {
                    pausedByTransientLoss = false
                    if (audioEngine.play()) {
                        wasPlaying = true
                        startProgressUpdates()
                        updateMediaSessionState(PlaybackState.STATE_PLAYING, audioEngine.getPositionMs())
                        notifyState(true)
                        showNotification()
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // --- Notifications & Control Center ---

    private fun createNotificationChannel() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            val channel = NotificationChannel(
                CHANNEL_ID,
                "Reprodução de Música",
                NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW
            ).apply {
                description = "Controles de reprodução do player"
                setShowBadge(false)
            }
            val nm = getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java)
            nm?.createNotificationChannel(channel)
        }
    }

    private fun buildNotification(): Notification {
        val track = queueManager.currentTrack
        val isPlaying = audioEngine.isPlaying()

        val mainIntent = Intent(this, MainActivity::class.java)
        val contentPendingIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            this,
            0,
            mainIntent,
            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        )

        val prevIntent = Intent(this, PlaybackService::class.java).apply { action = ACTION_PREVIOUS }
        val prevPending = PendingIntent.getService(
            this, 1, prevIntent, PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        )

        val toggleIntent = Intent(this, PlaybackService::class.java).apply { action = ACTION_TOGGLE_PLAY_PAUSE }
        val togglePending = PendingIntent.getService(
            this, 2, toggleIntent, PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        )

        val nextIntent = Intent(this, PlaybackService::class.java).apply { action = ACTION_NEXT }
        val nextPending = PendingIntent.getService(
            this, 3, nextIntent, PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        )

        val playPauseIcon = if (isPlaying) R.drawable.ic_notification_pause else R.drawable.ic_notification_play

        val builder = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            Notification.Builder(this, CHANNEL_ID)
        } else {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            Notification.Builder(this)
        }

        builder.setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_stat_player)
            .setContentTitle(track?.title ?: getString(R.string.app_name))
            .setContentText(track?.artist ?: "")
            .setSubText(track?.album)
            .setColor(currentAccentColor)
            .setContentIntent(contentPendingIntent)
            .setVisibility(Notification.VISIBILITY_PUBLIC)
            .setOngoing(isPlaying)
            .addAction(Notification.Action.Builder(R.drawable.ic_notification_prev, getString(R.string.notification_prev), prevPending).build())
            .addAction(Notification.Action.Builder(playPauseIcon, if (isPlaying) getString(R.string.notification_pause) else getString(R.string.notification_play), togglePending).build())
            .addAction(Notification.Action.Builder(R.drawable.ic_notification_next, getString(R.string.notification_next), nextPending).build())
            .setStyle(
                Notification.MediaStyle()
                    .setMediaSession(mediaSession.sessionToken)
                    .setShowActionsInCompactView(0, 1, 2)
            )

        currentCoverBitmap?.let {
            builder.setLargeIcon(it)
        }

        return builder.build()
    }

    private fun showNotification() {
        val nm = getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as NotificationManager
        nm.notify(NOTIFICATION_ID, buildNotification())
    }

    companion object {
        const val CHANNEL_ID = "player_playback_channel"
        const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 1001

        const val ACTION_PLAY = "com.lucas.player.ACTION_PLAY"
        const val ACTION_PAUSE = "com.lucas.player.ACTION_PAUSE"
        const val ACTION_TOGGLE_PLAY_PAUSE = "com.lucas.player.ACTION_TOGGLE_PLAY_PAUSE"
        const val ACTION_NEXT = "com.lucas.player.ACTION_NEXT"
        const val ACTION_PREVIOUS = "com.lucas.player.ACTION_PREVIOUS"
        const val ACTION_STOP = "com.lucas.player.ACTION_STOP"
    }
}


=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/lucas/player/queue/QueueManager.kt ===
package com.lucas.player.queue

import com.lucas.player.model.Track
import java.util.Collections

enum class RepeatMode(val value: Int, val displayName: String) {
    OFF(0, "Repetir: Desativado"),
    ONE(1, "Repetir: 1 Música"),
    CATEGORY(2, "Repetir: Álbum/Categoria"),
    ALL(3, "Repetir: Toda a Fila"),
    ADVANCE_CATEGORY(4, "Avançar Categoria");

    companion object {
        fun fromValue(value: Int): RepeatMode =
            values().firstOrNull { it.value == value } ?: OFF
    }
}

enum class ShuffleMode(val value: Int, val displayName: String) {
    OFF(0, "Aleatório: Desativado"),
    ALL_TRACKS(1, "Aleatório: Todas as Músicas"),
    WITHIN_CATEGORY(2, "Aleatório: Dentro da Categoria"),
    CATEGORIES(3, "Aleatório: Categorias"),
    SONGS_AND_CATEGORIES(4, "Aleatório: Músicas e Categorias");

    companion object {
        fun fromValue(value: Int): ShuffleMode =
            values().firstOrNull { it.value == value } ?: OFF
    }
}

enum class QueueSection {
    PRIORITY, // "A Seguir"
    QUEUE     // "Fila"
}

class QueueManager {

    data class PreQueueSnapshot(
        val tracks: List<Track>,
        val index: Int
    )

    private val originalTracks = mutableListOf<Track>()
    private val shuffleIndices = mutableListOf<Int>()
    private var currentIndex: Int = -1

    // Poweramp Priority Queue System
    private val priorityQueue = mutableListOf<Track>()
    private var preQueueSnapshot: PreQueueSnapshot? = null
    var isPlayingFromPriorityQueue: Boolean = false
        private set

    var shuffleMode: ShuffleMode = ShuffleMode.OFF
        private set

    val isShuffle: Boolean
        get() = shuffleMode != ShuffleMode.OFF

    var repeatMode: RepeatMode = RepeatMode.OFF
        private set

    fun setQueue(tracks: List<Track>, startIndex: Int = 0) {
        synchronized(this) {
            originalTracks.clear()
            originalTracks.addAll(tracks)

            if (originalTracks.isEmpty()) {
                currentIndex = -1
                shuffleIndices.clear()
                return
            }

            val safeIndex = startIndex.coerceIn(0, originalTracks.size - 1)
            rebuildShuffleIndices(preserveCurrentIndex = safeIndex)
            currentIndex = safeIndex
            isPlayingFromPriorityQueue = false
        }
    }

    val currentTrack: Track?
        get() = synchronized(this) {
            if (isPlayingFromPriorityQueue && priorityQueue.isNotEmpty()) {
                return priorityQueue.firstOrNull()
            }
            if (currentIndex in originalTracks.indices) {
                originalTracks[currentIndex]
            } else {
                null
            }
        }

    val currentQueueIndex: Int
        get() = synchronized(this) {
            if (isShuffle && shuffleIndices.size == originalTracks.size) {
                val pos = shuffleIndices.indexOf(currentIndex)
                if (pos != -1) pos else currentIndex
            } else {
                currentIndex
            }
        }

    val queueSize: Int
        get() = synchronized(this) { originalTracks.size }

    fun getTracks(): List<Track> = synchronized(this) {
        if (isShuffle && shuffleIndices.size == originalTracks.size) {
            shuffleIndices.mapNotNull { originalTracks.getOrNull(it) }
        } else {
            originalTracks.toList()
        }
    }

    fun getOriginalTracks(): List<Track> = synchronized(this) { originalTracks.toList() }

    fun getShuffleIndices(): List<Int> = synchronized(this) { shuffleIndices.toList() }

    internal fun setShuffleIndicesForTest(indices: List<Int>) {
        synchronized(this) {
            shuffleIndices.clear()
            shuffleIndices.addAll(indices)
        }
    }

    // --- Priority Queue (Poweramp Queue Engine) ---

    fun enqueueNext(track: Track) {
        synchronized(this) {
            if (priorityQueue.isEmpty()) {
                preQueueSnapshot = PreQueueSnapshot(originalTracks.toList(), currentIndex)
            }
            priorityQueue.add(0, track)
        }
    }

    fun enqueueEnd(track: Track) {
        synchronized(this) {
            if (priorityQueue.isEmpty()) {
                preQueueSnapshot = PreQueueSnapshot(originalTracks.toList(), currentIndex)
            }
            priorityQueue.add(track)
        }
    }

    fun enqueueList(tracks: List<Track>, playNext: Boolean) {
        synchronized(this) {
            if (priorityQueue.isEmpty()) {
                preQueueSnapshot = PreQueueSnapshot(originalTracks.toList(), currentIndex)
            }
            if (playNext) {
                priorityQueue.addAll(0, tracks)
            } else {
                priorityQueue.addAll(tracks)
            }
        }
    }

    fun getPriorityQueue(): List<Track> = synchronized(this) { priorityQueue.toList() }

    fun getUpcomingTracks(maxCount: Int = 2): List<Track> = synchronized(this) {
        val result = mutableListOf<Track>()
        if (priorityQueue.isNotEmpty()) {
            val startIdx = if (isPlayingFromPriorityQueue) 1 else 0
            for (i in startIdx until priorityQueue.size) {
                result.add(priorityQueue[i])
                if (result.size >= maxCount) return result
            }
        }
        if (originalTracks.isNotEmpty()) {
            val basePos = if (isShuffle) shuffleIndices.indexOf(currentIndex) else currentIndex
            val startIdx = basePos + 1
            for (i in startIdx until originalTracks.size) {
                val actualIndex = if (isShuffle && i in shuffleIndices.indices) shuffleIndices[i] else i
                if (actualIndex in originalTracks.indices) {
                    result.add(originalTracks[actualIndex])
                    if (result.size >= maxCount) return result
                }
            }
        }
        return result
    }

    fun clearPriorityQueue() {
        synchronized(this) {
            priorityQueue.clear()
            if (isPlayingFromPriorityQueue) {
                restorePreQueue()
            }
        }
    }

    fun skipToPriority(index: Int): Track? {
        synchronized(this) {
            if (index in priorityQueue.indices) {
                if (index > 0) {
                    priorityQueue.subList(0, index).clear()
                }
                isPlayingFromPriorityQueue = true
                return priorityQueue.firstOrNull()
            }
            return null
        }
    }

    private fun restorePreQueue() {
        preQueueSnapshot?.let { snapshot ->
            originalTracks.clear()
            originalTracks.addAll(snapshot.tracks)
            currentIndex = snapshot.index.coerceIn(0, (originalTracks.size - 1).coerceAtLeast(0))
            rebuildShuffleIndices(preserveCurrentIndex = currentIndex)
        }
        preQueueSnapshot = null
        isPlayingFromPriorityQueue = false
    }

    // --- Navigation Next / Previous ---

    fun peekNext(): Track? {
        synchronized(this) {
            if (repeatMode == RepeatMode.ONE) {
                return currentTrack
            }
            if (priorityQueue.isNotEmpty()) {
                if (isPlayingFromPriorityQueue) {
                    if (priorityQueue.size > 1) return priorityQueue[1]
                    return preQueueSnapshot?.let { snapshot ->
                        val nextIdx = snapshot.index + 1
                        if (nextIdx in snapshot.tracks.indices) snapshot.tracks[nextIdx]
                        else if (repeatMode == RepeatMode.ALL || repeatMode == RepeatMode.CATEGORY) snapshot.tracks.firstOrNull()
                        else null
                    }
                } else {
                    return priorityQueue.firstOrNull()
                }
            }
            if (originalTracks.isEmpty()) return null
            if (isShuffle) {
                val currentShufflePos = shuffleIndices.indexOf(currentIndex)
                val nextShufflePos = currentShufflePos + 1
                if (nextShufflePos < shuffleIndices.size) {
                    return originalTracks[shuffleIndices[nextShufflePos]]
                } else if (repeatMode == RepeatMode.ALL || repeatMode == RepeatMode.CATEGORY) {
                    return originalTracks[shuffleIndices.firstOrNull() ?: 0]
                }
                return null
            } else {
                val nextIndex = currentIndex + 1
                if (nextIndex < originalTracks.size) {
                    return originalTracks[nextIndex]
                } else if (repeatMode == RepeatMode.ALL || repeatMode == RepeatMode.CATEGORY) {
                    return originalTracks.firstOrNull()
                }
                return null
            }
        }
    }

    fun next(): Track? {
        synchronized(this) {
            if (repeatMode == RepeatMode.ONE) {
                return currentTrack
            }

            // 1. Check if Priority Queue has items
            if (priorityQueue.isNotEmpty()) {
                // Remove the one that just played if we were already in priority queue
                if (isPlayingFromPriorityQueue) {
                    priorityQueue.removeAt(0)
                }

                if (priorityQueue.isNotEmpty()) {
                    isPlayingFromPriorityQueue = true
                    return priorityQueue[0]
                } else {
                    // Priority queue just emptied: seamless restore to original category!
                    restorePreQueue()
                }
            }

            if (originalTracks.isEmpty()) return null

            // 2. Standard Category Playback
            if (isShuffle) {
                val currentShufflePos = shuffleIndices.indexOf(currentIndex)
                val nextShufflePos = currentShufflePos + 1
                if (nextShufflePos < shuffleIndices.size) {
                    currentIndex = shuffleIndices[nextShufflePos]
                    return originalTracks[currentIndex]
                } else if (repeatMode == RepeatMode.ALL || repeatMode == RepeatMode.CATEGORY) {
                    rebuildShuffleIndices(preserveCurrentIndex = -1)
                    currentIndex = shuffleIndices.firstOrNull() ?: 0
                    return originalTracks[currentIndex]
                } else {
                    return null // Reached end of shuffled queue
                }
            } else {
                val nextIndex = currentIndex + 1
                if (nextIndex < originalTracks.size) {
                    currentIndex = nextIndex
                    return originalTracks[currentIndex]
                } else if (repeatMode == RepeatMode.ALL || repeatMode == RepeatMode.CATEGORY) {
                    currentIndex = 0
                    return originalTracks[currentIndex]
                } else {
                    return null // Reached end of linear queue
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun previous(): Track? {
        synchronized(this) {
            if (repeatMode == RepeatMode.ONE) {
                return currentTrack
            }

            if (isPlayingFromPriorityQueue && priorityQueue.isNotEmpty()) {
                return priorityQueue.firstOrNull()
            }

            if (originalTracks.isEmpty()) return null

            if (isShuffle) {
                val currentShufflePos = shuffleIndices.indexOf(currentIndex)
                val prevShufflePos = currentShufflePos - 1
                if (prevShufflePos >= 0) {
                    currentIndex = shuffleIndices[prevShufflePos]
                    return originalTracks[currentIndex]
                } else if (repeatMode == RepeatMode.ALL || repeatMode == RepeatMode.CATEGORY) {
                    currentIndex = shuffleIndices.lastOrNull() ?: 0
                    return originalTracks[currentIndex]
                } else {
                    return originalTracks[shuffleIndices[0]]
                }
            } else {
                val prevIndex = currentIndex - 1
                if (prevIndex >= 0) {
                    currentIndex = prevIndex
                    return originalTracks[currentIndex]
                } else if (repeatMode == RepeatMode.ALL || repeatMode == RepeatMode.CATEGORY) {
                    currentIndex = originalTracks.size - 1
                    return originalTracks[currentIndex]
                } else {
                    return originalTracks[0]
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun skipTo(index: Int): Track? {
        synchronized(this) {
            if (isShuffle && index in shuffleIndices.indices) {
                currentIndex = shuffleIndices[index]
                isPlayingFromPriorityQueue = false
                return originalTracks[currentIndex]
            }
            if (index in originalTracks.indices) {
                currentIndex = index
                isPlayingFromPriorityQueue = false
                return originalTracks[currentIndex]
            }
            return null
        }
    }

    // --- Reordenação da Fila (Fase 4.1) ---

    fun moveItem(section: QueueSection, from: Int, to: Int): Boolean {
        synchronized(this) {
            when (section) {
                QueueSection.PRIORITY -> {
                    if (from !in priorityQueue.indices || to !in priorityQueue.indices) return false
                    if (from == to) return true
                    val item = priorityQueue.removeAt(from)
                    priorityQueue.add(to, item)
                    return true
                }
                QueueSection.QUEUE -> {
                    if (isShuffle) {
                        if (from !in shuffleIndices.indices || to !in shuffleIndices.indices) return false
                        if (from == to) return true
                        val idx = shuffleIndices.removeAt(from)
                        shuffleIndices.add(to, idx)
                        return true
                    } else {
                        if (from !in originalTracks.indices || to !in originalTracks.indices) return false
                        if (from == to) return true
                        val item = originalTracks.removeAt(from)
                        originalTracks.add(to, item)

                        if (from == currentIndex) {
                            currentIndex = to
                        } else if (from < currentIndex && to >= currentIndex) {
                            currentIndex--
                        } else if (from > currentIndex && to <= currentIndex) {
                            currentIndex++
                        }

                        if (shuffleIndices.isNotEmpty()) {
                            rebuildShuffleIndices(preserveCurrentIndex = currentIndex)
                        }
                        return true
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun moveItem(section: String, from: Int, to: Int): Boolean {
        val sec = when (section.trim().lowercase()) {
            "a seguir", "priority", "prioritária", "prioritaria" -> QueueSection.PRIORITY
            "fila", "queue", "normal", "regular" -> QueueSection.QUEUE
            else -> return false
        }
        return moveItem(sec, from, to)
    }

    // --- Shuffle Modes ---

    fun toggleShuffle(): ShuffleMode {
        synchronized(this) {
            shuffleMode = when (shuffleMode) {
                ShuffleMode.OFF -> ShuffleMode.ALL_TRACKS
                ShuffleMode.ALL_TRACKS -> ShuffleMode.WITHIN_CATEGORY
                ShuffleMode.WITHIN_CATEGORY -> ShuffleMode.OFF
                else -> ShuffleMode.OFF
            }
            if (isShuffle) {
                rebuildShuffleIndices(preserveCurrentIndex = currentIndex)
            }
            return shuffleMode
        }
    }

    fun setShuffleMode(mode: ShuffleMode) {
        synchronized(this) {
            shuffleMode = mode
            if (isShuffle) {
                rebuildShuffleIndices(preserveCurrentIndex = currentIndex)
            }
        }
    }

    // --- Repeat Modes ---

    fun toggleRepeat(): RepeatMode {
        synchronized(this) {
            repeatMode = when (repeatMode) {
                RepeatMode.OFF -> RepeatMode.ALL
                RepeatMode.ALL -> RepeatMode.CATEGORY
                RepeatMode.CATEGORY -> RepeatMode.ONE
                RepeatMode.ONE -> RepeatMode.ADVANCE_CATEGORY
                RepeatMode.ADVANCE_CATEGORY -> RepeatMode.OFF
            }
            return repeatMode
        }
    }

    fun setRepeat(mode: RepeatMode) {
        synchronized(this) {
            repeatMode = mode
        }
    }

    fun removePriorityQueueItem(index: Int): Boolean {
        synchronized(this) {
            if (index in priorityQueue.indices) {
                priorityQueue.removeAt(index)
                if (priorityQueue.isEmpty() && isPlayingFromPriorityQueue) {
                    restorePreQueue()
                }
                return true
            }
            return false
        }
    }

    fun removeTrackById(trackId: Long): Boolean {
        synchronized(this) {
            val pqIdx = priorityQueue.indexOfFirst { it.id == trackId }
            if (pqIdx != -1) {
                return removePriorityQueueItem(pqIdx)
            }
            val origIdx = originalTracks.indexOfFirst { it.id == trackId }
            if (origIdx != -1) {
                originalTracks.removeAt(origIdx)
                if (origIdx < currentIndex) {
                    currentIndex--
                } else if (origIdx == currentIndex && currentIndex >= originalTracks.size) {
                    currentIndex = (originalTracks.size - 1).coerceAtLeast(0)
                }
                if (originalTracks.isEmpty()) {
                    currentIndex = -1
                }
                rebuildShuffleIndices(preserveCurrentIndex = currentIndex)
                return true
            }
            return false
        }
    }

    fun clearAllQueue() {
        synchronized(this) {
            priorityQueue.clear()
            originalTracks.clear()
            currentIndex = -1
            shuffleIndices.clear()
            preQueueSnapshot = null
            isPlayingFromPriorityQueue = false
        }
    }

    fun shuffleQueue() {
        synchronized(this) {
            if (originalTracks.isNotEmpty()) {
                rebuildShuffleIndices(preserveCurrentIndex = currentIndex)
            }
            if (priorityQueue.isNotEmpty()) {
                priorityQueue.shuffle()
            }
        }
    }

    private fun rebuildShuffleIndices(preserveCurrentIndex: Int) {
        shuffleIndices.clear()
        if (originalTracks.isEmpty()) return

        val indices = (originalTracks.indices).toMutableList()
        if (preserveCurrentIndex in indices) {
            indices.remove(preserveCurrentIndex)
            Collections.shuffle(indices)
            shuffleIndices.add(preserveCurrentIndex)
            shuffleIndices.addAll(indices)
        } else {
            Collections.shuffle(indices)
            shuffleIndices.addAll(indices)
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/lucas/player/data/MusicDatabase.kt ===
package com.lucas.player.data

import android.content.ContentValues
import android.content.Context
import android.database.sqlite.SQLiteDatabase
import android.database.sqlite.SQLiteOpenHelper

/**
 * Lightweight native SQLite Database managing track statistics, ratings, and playlists
 * replicating Poweramp's database persistence model.
 */
class MusicDatabase(context: Context) : SQLiteOpenHelper(context, DATABASE_NAME, null, DATABASE_VERSION) {

    data class TrackStat(
        val trackId: Long,
        val playCount: Int,
        val lastPlayedMs: Long,
        val rating: Int
    )

    data class Playlist(
        val id: Long,
        val name: String,
        val createdMs: Long,
        val trackCount: Int = 0
    )

    data class EqPreset(
        val id: Long = 0,
        val name: String,
        val bandGains: List<Float>,
        val bassGain: Float = 0f,
        val trebleGain: Float = 0f,
        val preampGain: Float = 0f,
        val isBuiltin: Boolean = false
    )

    override fun onCreate(db: SQLiteDatabase) {
        db.execSQL(
            """
            CREATE TABLE $TABLE_STATS (
                $COL_TRACK_ID INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                $COL_PLAY_COUNT INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                $COL_LAST_PLAYED INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                $COL_RATING INTEGER DEFAULT 0
            )
            """.trimIndent()
        )

        db.execSQL(
            """
            CREATE TABLE $TABLE_PLAYLISTS (
                $COL_PL_ID INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                $COL_PL_NAME TEXT NOT NULL,
                $COL_PL_CREATED INTEGER NOT NULL
            )
            """.trimIndent()
        )

        db.execSQL(
            """
            CREATE TABLE $TABLE_PLAYLIST_ITEMS (
                $COL_ITEM_PL_ID INTEGER NOT NULL,
                $COL_ITEM_TRACK_ID INTEGER NOT NULL,
                $COL_ITEM_ORDER INTEGER NOT NULL,
                PRIMARY KEY ($COL_ITEM_PL_ID, $COL_ITEM_TRACK_ID)
            )
            """.trimIndent()
        )

        createPresetsTable(db)
        seedBuiltinPresets(db)
    }

    override fun onUpgrade(db: SQLiteDatabase, oldVersion: Int, newVersion: Int) {
        if (oldVersion < 2) {
            createPresetsTable(db)
            seedBuiltinPresets(db)
        }
    }

    private fun createPresetsTable(db: SQLiteDatabase) {
        db.execSQL(
            """
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS $TABLE_PRESETS (
                $COL_PRESET_ID INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                $COL_PRESET_NAME TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                $COL_PRESET_GAINS TEXT NOT NULL,
                $COL_PRESET_BASS REAL DEFAULT 0,
                $COL_PRESET_TREBLE REAL DEFAULT 0,
                $COL_PRESET_PREAMP REAL DEFAULT 0,
                $COL_PRESET_BUILTIN INTEGER DEFAULT 0
            )
            """.trimIndent()
        )
    }

    private fun seedBuiltinPresets(db: SQLiteDatabase) {
        val builtins = listOf(
            EqPreset(name = "Flat", bandGains = List(10) { 0f }, bassGain = 0f, trebleGain = 0f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Bass Heavy", bandGains = listOf(8f, 7f, 5f, 2f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 2f, 3f), bassGain = 5f, trebleGain = 0f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Rock", bandGains = listOf(5f, 4f, 2f, -1f, -2f, 1f, 3f, 5f, 6f, 6f), bassGain = 3f, trebleGain = 3f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Pop", bandGains = listOf(-1f, 1f, 3f, 5f, 4f, 2f, -1f, 1f, 3f, 4f), bassGain = 2f, trebleGain = 2f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Jazz", bandGains = listOf(3f, 3f, 2f, 1f, 1f, 2f, 2f, 3f, 4f, 4f), bassGain = 2f, trebleGain = 2f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Vocal Boost", bandGains = listOf(-3f, -2f, 0f, 2f, 5f, 5f, 3f, 1f, 0f, -1f), bassGain = -1f, trebleGain = 1f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Electronic", bandGains = listOf(6f, 5f, 2f, 0f, -2f, 2f, 1f, 3f, 5f, 6f), bassGain = 5f, trebleGain = 4f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Classical", bandGains = listOf(4f, 3f, 2f, 1f, -1f, -1f, 0f, 2f, 4f, 4f), bassGain = 1f, trebleGain = 2f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Dance", bandGains = listOf(6f, 6f, 3f, 0f, 1f, 2f, 4f, 5f, 4f, 0f), bassGain = 6f, trebleGain = 2f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Acoustic", bandGains = listOf(4f, 3f, 1f, 1f, 2f, 2f, 3f, 3f, 4f, 3f), bassGain = 2f, trebleGain = 3f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Deep Bass", bandGains = listOf(10f, 8f, 5f, 2f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f), bassGain = 7f, trebleGain = 0f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Techno", bandGains = listOf(5f, 4f, 0f, -2f, -1f, 1f, 4f, 5f, 5f, 3f), bassGain = 4f, trebleGain = 3f, isBuiltin = true)
        )

        for (p in builtins) {
            val cv = ContentValues().apply {
                put(COL_PRESET_NAME, p.name)
                put(COL_PRESET_GAINS, p.bandGains.joinToString(","))
                put(COL_PRESET_BASS, p.bassGain)
                put(COL_PRESET_TREBLE, p.trebleGain)
                put(COL_PRESET_PREAMP, p.preampGain)
                put(COL_PRESET_BUILTIN, 1)
            }
            db.insertWithOnConflict(TABLE_PRESETS, null, cv, SQLiteDatabase.CONFLICT_IGNORE)
        }
    }

    // --- Statistics & Ratings ---

    fun incrementPlayCount(trackId: Long) {
        val db = writableDatabase
        db.execSQL(
            """
            INSERT INTO $TABLE_STATS ($COL_TRACK_ID, $COL_PLAY_COUNT, $COL_LAST_PLAYED, $COL_RATING)
            VALUES (?, 1, ?, 0)
            ON CONFLICT($COL_TRACK_ID) DO UPDATE SET
                $COL_PLAY_COUNT = $COL_PLAY_COUNT + 1,
                $COL_LAST_PLAYED = ?
            """.trimIndent(),
            arrayOf(trackId, System.currentTimeMillis(), System.currentTimeMillis())
        )
    }

    fun setRating(trackId: Long, rating: Int) {
        val db = writableDatabase
        db.execSQL(
            """
            INSERT INTO $TABLE_STATS ($COL_TRACK_ID, $COL_PLAY_COUNT, $COL_LAST_PLAYED, $COL_RATING)
            VALUES (?, 0, 0, ?)
            ON CONFLICT($COL_TRACK_ID) DO UPDATE SET
                $COL_RATING = ?
            """.trimIndent(),
            arrayOf(trackId, rating, rating)
        )
    }

    fun getAllStats(): Map<Long, TrackStat> {
        val result = mutableMapOf<Long, TrackStat>()
        val db = readableDatabase
        db.rawQuery("SELECT $COL_TRACK_ID, $COL_PLAY_COUNT, $COL_LAST_PLAYED, $COL_RATING FROM $TABLE_STATS", null).use { cursor ->
            val idCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_TRACK_ID)
            val pcCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PLAY_COUNT)
            val lpCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_LAST_PLAYED)
            val ratCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_RATING)

            while (cursor.moveToNext()) {
                val id = cursor.getLong(idCol)
                val pc = cursor.getInt(pcCol)
                val lp = cursor.getLong(lpCol)
                val rat = cursor.getInt(ratCol)
                result[id] = TrackStat(id, pc, lp, rat)
            }
        }
        return result
    }

    // --- Playlists Management ---

    fun createPlaylist(name: String): Long {
        val db = writableDatabase
        val values = ContentValues().apply {
            put(COL_PL_NAME, name)
            put(COL_PL_CREATED, System.currentTimeMillis())
        }
        return db.insert(TABLE_PLAYLISTS, null, values)
    }

    fun getPlaylists(): List<Playlist> {
        val list = mutableListOf<Playlist>()
        val db = readableDatabase
        val query = """
            SELECT p.$COL_PL_ID, p.$COL_PL_NAME, p.$COL_PL_CREATED, COUNT(i.$COL_ITEM_TRACK_ID) AS track_count
            FROM $TABLE_PLAYLISTS p
            LEFT JOIN $TABLE_PLAYLIST_ITEMS i ON p.$COL_PL_ID = i.$COL_ITEM_PL_ID
            GROUP BY p.$COL_PL_ID
            ORDER BY p.$COL_PL_NAME COLLATE NOCASE ASC
        """.trimIndent()

        db.rawQuery(query, null).use { cursor ->
            val idCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PL_ID)
            val nameCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PL_NAME)
            val crCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PL_CREATED)
            val cntCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow("track_count")

            while (cursor.moveToNext()) {
                list.add(
                    Playlist(
                        id = cursor.getLong(idCol),
                        name = cursor.getString(nameCol),
                        createdMs = cursor.getLong(crCol),
                        trackCount = cursor.getInt(cntCol)
                    )
                )
            }
        }
        return list
    }

    fun addTrackToPlaylist(playlistId: Long, trackId: Long) {
        val db = writableDatabase
        val countQuery = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $TABLE_PLAYLIST_ITEMS WHERE $COL_ITEM_PL_ID = ?"
        var nextOrder = 0
        db.rawQuery(countQuery, arrayOf(playlistId.toString())).use {
            if (it.moveToNext()) {
                nextOrder = it.getInt(0)
            }
        }

        val values = ContentValues().apply {
            put(COL_ITEM_PL_ID, playlistId)
            put(COL_ITEM_TRACK_ID, trackId)
            put(COL_ITEM_ORDER, nextOrder)
        }
        db.insertWithOnConflict(TABLE_PLAYLIST_ITEMS, null, values, SQLiteDatabase.CONFLICT_REPLACE)
    }

    fun getTracksForPlaylist(playlistId: Long): List<Long> {
        val list = mutableListOf<Long>()
        val db = readableDatabase
        val query = "SELECT $COL_ITEM_TRACK_ID FROM $TABLE_PLAYLIST_ITEMS WHERE $COL_ITEM_PL_ID = ? ORDER BY $COL_ITEM_ORDER ASC"
        db.rawQuery(query, arrayOf(playlistId.toString())).use { cursor ->
            val idCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_ITEM_TRACK_ID)
            while (cursor.moveToNext()) {
                list.add(cursor.getLong(idCol))
            }
        }
        return list
    }

    fun removeTrackFromPlaylist(playlistId: Long, trackId: Long) {
        val db = writableDatabase
        db.delete(TABLE_PLAYLIST_ITEMS, "$COL_ITEM_PL_ID = ? AND $COL_ITEM_TRACK_ID = ?", arrayOf(playlistId.toString(), trackId.toString()))
    }

    fun deletePlaylist(playlistId: Long) {
        val db = writableDatabase
        db.delete(TABLE_PLAYLIST_ITEMS, "$COL_ITEM_PL_ID = ?", arrayOf(playlistId.toString()))
        db.delete(TABLE_PLAYLISTS, "$COL_PL_ID = ?", arrayOf(playlistId.toString()))
    }

    // --- Equalizer Presets ---

    fun savePreset(name: String, bandGains: List<Float>, bass: Float, treble: Float, preamp: Float): Long {
        val db = writableDatabase
        val cv = ContentValues().apply {
            put(COL_PRESET_NAME, name.trim())
            put(COL_PRESET_GAINS, bandGains.joinToString(","))
            put(COL_PRESET_BASS, bass)
            put(COL_PRESET_TREBLE, treble)
            put(COL_PRESET_PREAMP, preamp)
            put(COL_PRESET_BUILTIN, 0)
        }
        return db.insertWithOnConflict(TABLE_PRESETS, null, cv, SQLiteDatabase.CONFLICT_REPLACE)
    }

    fun getAllPresets(): List<EqPreset> {
        val list = mutableListOf<EqPreset>()
        val db = readableDatabase
        val query = "SELECT * FROM $TABLE_PRESETS ORDER BY $COL_PRESET_BUILTIN DESC, $COL_PRESET_NAME ASC"
        db.rawQuery(query, null).use { cursor ->
            val idCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PRESET_ID)
            val nameCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PRESET_NAME)
            val gainsCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PRESET_GAINS)
            val bassCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PRESET_BASS)
            val trebleCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PRESET_TREBLE)
            val preampCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PRESET_PREAMP)
            val builtinCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PRESET_BUILTIN)

            while (cursor.moveToNext()) {
                val gainsStr = cursor.getString(gainsCol)
                val gains = gainsStr.split(",").mapNotNull { it.toFloatOrNull() }
                val validGains = if (gains.size == 10) gains else List(10) { 0f }
                list.add(
                    EqPreset(
                        id = cursor.getLong(idCol),
                        name = cursor.getString(nameCol),
                        bandGains = validGains,
                        bassGain = cursor.getFloat(bassCol),
                        trebleGain = cursor.getFloat(trebleCol),
                        preampGain = cursor.getFloat(preampCol),
                        isBuiltin = cursor.getInt(builtinCol) == 1
                    )
                )
            }
        }
        return list
    }

    fun deletePreset(presetId: Long): Boolean {
        val db = writableDatabase
        val rows = db.delete(TABLE_PRESETS, "$COL_PRESET_ID = ? AND $COL_PRESET_BUILTIN = 0", arrayOf(presetId.toString()))
        return rows > 0
    }

    companion object {
        const val DATABASE_NAME = "poweramp_library.db"
        const val DATABASE_VERSION = 2

        const val TABLE_STATS = "track_stats"
        const val COL_TRACK_ID = "track_id"
        const val COL_PLAY_COUNT = "play_count"
        const val COL_LAST_PLAYED = "last_played_ms"
        const val COL_RATING = "rating"

        const val TABLE_PLAYLISTS = "playlists"
        const val COL_PL_ID = "id"
        const val COL_PL_NAME = "name"
        const val COL_PL_CREATED = "created_ms"

        const val TABLE_PLAYLIST_ITEMS = "playlist_items"
        const val COL_ITEM_PL_ID = "playlist_id"
        const val COL_ITEM_TRACK_ID = "track_id"
        const val COL_ITEM_ORDER = "sort_order"

        const val TABLE_PRESETS = "eq_presets"
        const val COL_PRESET_ID = "id"
        const val COL_PRESET_NAME = "name"
        const val COL_PRESET_GAINS = "gains"
        const val COL_PRESET_BASS = "bass"
        const val COL_PRESET_TREBLE = "treble"
        const val COL_PRESET_PREAMP = "preamp"
        const val COL_PRESET_BUILTIN = "is_builtin"
    }
}


=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/lucas/player/data/LibraryRepository.kt ===
package com.lucas.player.data

import android.content.ContentUris
import android.content.Context
import android.os.Build
import android.provider.MediaStore
import com.lucas.player.model.*
import java.io.File
import java.util.concurrent.Executors

class LibraryRepository() {

    companion object {
        @Volatile
        private var instance: LibraryRepository? = null

        private val sharedTracks = mutableListOf<Track>()
        @Volatile
        private var sharedMusicDb: MusicDatabase? = null
        @Volatile
        private var appContext: Context? = null
        private val sharedExecutor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor()

        fun getInstance(context: Context? = null): LibraryRepository {
            if (context != null && appContext == null) {
                init(context)
            }
            return instance ?: synchronized(this) {
                instance ?: LibraryRepository().also { instance = it }
            }
        }

        fun init(context: Context) {
            if (appContext == null) {
                appContext = context.applicationContext
            }
            if (sharedMusicDb == null) {
                sharedMusicDb = MusicDatabase(context.applicationContext)
            }
        }

        private fun getOrCreateDb(context: Context? = null): MusicDatabase? {
            if (sharedMusicDb == null) {
                val ctx = context ?: appContext
                if (ctx != null) {
                    sharedMusicDb = MusicDatabase(ctx.applicationContext)
                }
            }
            return sharedMusicDb
        }
    }

    fun loadTracks(context: Context, onComplete: (List<Track>) -> Unit) {
        init(context)
        sharedExecutor.execute {
            val db = getOrCreateDb(context)
            val statsMap = db?.getAllStats() ?: emptyMap()

            val list = queryMediaStore(context, statsMap)
            synchronized(sharedTracks) {
                sharedTracks.clear()
                sharedTracks.addAll(list)
            }
            onComplete(list)
        }
    }

    fun getAllTracks(): List<Track> {
        return synchronized(sharedTracks) { sharedTracks.toList() }
    }

    fun recordTrackPlayed(trackId: Long) {
        sharedExecutor.execute {
            getOrCreateDb()?.incrementPlayCount(trackId)
            synchronized(sharedTracks) {
                val index = sharedTracks.indexOfFirst { it.id == trackId }
                if (index != -1) {
                    val t = sharedTracks[index]
                    sharedTracks[index] = t.copy(
                        playCount = t.playCount + 1,
                        lastPlayedTimestamp = System.currentTimeMillis()
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun setTrackRating(trackId: Long, rating: Int) {
        sharedExecutor.execute {
            getOrCreateDb()?.setRating(trackId, rating)
            synchronized(sharedTracks) {
                val index = sharedTracks.indexOfFirst { it.id == trackId }
                if (index != -1) {
                    val t = sharedTracks[index]
                    sharedTracks[index] = t.copy(rating = rating)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun ensureReplayGainLoaded(track: Track): Track {
        if (track.path.isEmpty() || track.replayGainTrackGain != 0.0f || track.replayGainTrackPeak != 1.0f) {
            return track
        }
        val rgInfo = ReplayGainReader.readFromFile(track.path)
        if (!rgInfo.hasData) return track
        val updated = track.copy(
            replayGainTrackGain = rgInfo.trackGainDb,
            replayGainTrackPeak = rgInfo.trackPeak,
            replayGainAlbumGain = rgInfo.albumGainDb,
            replayGainAlbumPeak = rgInfo.albumPeak
        )
        synchronized(sharedTracks) {
            val idx = sharedTracks.indexOfFirst { it.id == track.id }
            if (idx != -1) {
                sharedTracks[idx] = updated
            }
        }
        return updated
    }

    // --- Categorias e Agrupamentos ---

    fun getAlbums(): List<Album> {
        val all = getAllTracks()
        return all.groupBy { it.album }
            .map { (albumName, albumTracks) ->
                val artist = albumTracks.firstOrNull()?.artist ?: "<Desconhecido>"
                val year = albumTracks.map { it.year }.filter { it > 0 }.minOrNull() ?: 0
                Album(albumName, artist, albumTracks.size, year)
            }
            .sortedBy { it.name.lowercase() }
    }

    fun getTracksForAlbum(albumName: String, sortMode: SortMode = SortMode.TRACK_NUMBER): List<Track> {
        val filtered = getAllTracks().filter { it.album.equals(albumName, ignoreCase = true) }
        return applySorting(filtered, sortMode)
    }

    fun getArtists(): List<Artist> {
        val all = getAllTracks()
        return all.groupBy { it.artist }
            .map { (artistName, artistTracks) ->
                Artist(artistName, artistTracks.size)
            }
            .sortedBy { it.name.lowercase() }
    }

    fun getTracksForArtist(artistName: String, sortMode: SortMode = SortMode.ALBUM_ASC): List<Track> {
        val filtered = getAllTracks().filter { it.artist.equals(artistName, ignoreCase = true) }
        return applySorting(filtered, sortMode)
    }

    fun getAlbumArtists(): List<Artist> {
        val all = getAllTracks()
        return all.groupBy { if (it.albumArtist.isNotEmpty()) it.albumArtist else it.artist }
            .map { (name, list) ->
                Artist(name, list.size)
            }
            .sortedBy { it.name.lowercase() }
    }

    fun getTracksForAlbumArtist(albumArtist: String, sortMode: SortMode = SortMode.YEAR_DESC): List<Track> {
        val filtered = getAllTracks().filter {
            (if (it.albumArtist.isNotEmpty()) it.albumArtist else it.artist).equals(albumArtist, ignoreCase = true)
        }
        return applySorting(filtered, sortMode)
    }

    fun getFolders(): List<Folder> {
        val all = getAllTracks()
        return all.groupBy { it.folder }
            .map { (folderName, folderTracks) ->
                val path = folderTracks.firstOrNull()?.path ?: ""
                val parentPath = if (path.isNotEmpty()) File(path).parent ?: "" else ""
                Folder(folderName, parentPath, folderTracks.size)
            }
            .sortedBy { it.name.lowercase() }
    }

    fun getTracksForFolder(folderName: String, sortMode: SortMode = SortMode.TITLE_ASC): List<Track> {
        val filtered = getAllTracks().filter { it.folder.equals(folderName, ignoreCase = true) }
        return applySorting(filtered, sortMode)
    }

    fun getGenres(): List<Genre> {
        val all = getAllTracks()
        return all.filter { it.genre.isNotEmpty() }
            .groupBy { it.genre }
            .map { (genreName, tracks) ->
                Genre(genreName, tracks.size)
            }
            .sortedBy { it.name.lowercase() }
    }

    fun getTracksForGenre(genreName: String, sortMode: SortMode = SortMode.TITLE_ASC): List<Track> {
        val filtered = getAllTracks().filter { it.genre.equals(genreName, ignoreCase = true) }
        return applySorting(filtered, sortMode)
    }

    fun getYears(): List<YearGroup> {
        val all = getAllTracks()
        return all.filter { it.year > 0 }
            .groupBy { it.year }
            .map { (year, tracks) ->
                YearGroup(year, tracks.size)
            }
            .sortedByDescending { it.year }
    }

    fun getTracksForYear(year: Int, sortMode: SortMode = SortMode.TITLE_ASC): List<Track> {
        val filtered = getAllTracks().filter { it.year == year }
        return applySorting(filtered, sortMode)
    }

    fun getComposers(): List<Composer> {
        val all = getAllTracks()
        return all.filter { it.composer.isNotEmpty() }
            .groupBy { it.composer }
            .map { (name, tracks) ->
                Composer(name, tracks.size)
            }
            .sortedBy { it.name.lowercase() }
    }

    fun getTracksForComposer(composerName: String, sortMode: SortMode = SortMode.TITLE_ASC): List<Track> {
        val filtered = getAllTracks().filter { it.composer.equals(composerName, ignoreCase = true) }
        return applySorting(filtered, sortMode)
    }

    fun getRecentlyAdded(): List<Track> {
        return getAllTracks().sortedByDescending { it.dateAddedSec }
    }

    fun getRecentlyPlayed(): List<Track> {
        return getAllTracks()
            .filter { it.lastPlayedTimestamp > 0 }
            .sortedByDescending { it.lastPlayedTimestamp }
    }

    fun getMostPlayed(): List<Track> {
        return getAllTracks()
            .filter { it.playCount > 0 }
            .sortedByDescending { it.playCount }
    }

    fun getTopRated(): List<Track> {
        return getAllTracks()
            .filter { it.rating > 0 }
            .sortedByDescending { it.rating }
    }

    // --- Playlists ---

    fun getUserPlaylists(): List<UserPlaylist> {
        val db = getOrCreateDb() ?: return emptyList()
        return db.getPlaylists().map {
            UserPlaylist(it.id, it.name, it.trackCount, it.createdMs)
        }
    }

    fun createPlaylist(name: String): Long {
        return getOrCreateDb()?.createPlaylist(name) ?: -1L
    }

    fun getTracksForUserPlaylist(playlistId: Long): List<Track> {
        val db = getOrCreateDb() ?: return emptyList()
        val trackIds = db.getTracksForPlaylist(playlistId)
        val all = getAllTracks().associateBy { it.id }
        return trackIds.mapNotNull { all[it] }
    }

    fun addTrackToUserPlaylist(playlistId: Long, trackId: Long) {
        getOrCreateDb()?.addTrackToPlaylist(playlistId, trackId)
    }

    fun removeTrackFromUserPlaylist(playlistId: Long, trackId: Long) {
        getOrCreateDb()?.removeTrackFromPlaylist(playlistId, trackId)
    }

    fun deletePlaylist(playlistId: Long) {
        getOrCreateDb()?.deletePlaylist(playlistId)
    }

    // --- Ordenação Dinâmica ---

    fun applySorting(inputList: List<Track>, sortMode: SortMode): List<Track> {
        return when (sortMode) {
            SortMode.TITLE_ASC -> inputList.sortedBy { it.title.lowercase() }
            SortMode.TITLE_DESC -> inputList.sortedByDescending { it.title.lowercase() }
            SortMode.ARTIST_ASC -> inputList.sortedWith(compareBy({ it.artist.lowercase() }, { it.album.lowercase() }, { it.trackNumber }))
            SortMode.ALBUM_ASC -> inputList.sortedWith(compareBy({ it.album.lowercase() }, { it.trackNumber }, { it.title.lowercase() }))
            SortMode.YEAR_DESC -> inputList.sortedByDescending { it.year }
            SortMode.YEAR_ASC -> inputList.sortedBy { it.year }
            SortMode.TRACK_NUMBER -> inputList.sortedWith(compareBy({ it.discNumber }, { it.trackNumber }, { it.title.lowercase() }))
            SortMode.DURATION_DESC -> inputList.sortedByDescending { it.durationMs }
            SortMode.DURATION_ASC -> inputList.sortedBy { it.durationMs }
            SortMode.DATE_ADDED_DESC -> inputList.sortedByDescending { it.dateAddedSec }
            SortMode.PLAY_COUNT_DESC -> inputList.sortedByDescending { it.playCount }
            SortMode.RATING_DESC -> inputList.sortedByDescending { it.rating }
        }
    }

    // --- Query MediaStore ---

    private fun queryMediaStore(context: Context, statsMap: Map<Long, MusicDatabase.TrackStat>): List<Track> {
        val result = mutableListOf<Track>()
        val uri = MediaStore.Audio.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI

        val projectionList = mutableListOf(
            MediaStore.Audio.Media._ID,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.TITLE,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.ARTIST,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.ALBUM,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.DURATION,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.DATA,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.YEAR,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.TRACK,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.COMPOSER,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.DATE_ADDED,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.DATE_MODIFIED,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.SIZE
        )

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R) {
            projectionList.add(MediaStore.Audio.Media.ALBUM_ARTIST)
            projectionList.add(MediaStore.Audio.Media.GENRE)
        }

        val projection = projectionList.toTypedArray()
        val minSec = PreferencesManager(context).getIgnoreShorterThanSeconds()
        val selection = if (minSec > 0) {
            "${MediaStore.Audio.Media.IS_MUSIC} != 0 AND ${MediaStore.Audio.Media.DURATION} >= ${minSec * 1000L}"
        } else {
            "${MediaStore.Audio.Media.IS_MUSIC} != 0"
        }
        val sortOrder = "${MediaStore.Audio.Media.TITLE} COLLATE NOCASE ASC"

        try {
            context.contentResolver.query(uri, projection, selection, null, sortOrder)?.use { cursor ->
                val idCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.Audio.Media._ID)
                val titleCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.Audio.Media.TITLE)
                val artistCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.Audio.Media.ARTIST)
                val albumCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.Audio.Media.ALBUM)
                val durCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.Audio.Media.DURATION)
                val dataCol = cursor.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.DATA)
                val yearCol = cursor.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.YEAR)
                val trackCol = cursor.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.TRACK)
                val compCol = cursor.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.COMPOSER)
                val daCol = cursor.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.DATE_ADDED)
                val dmCol = cursor.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.DATE_MODIFIED)
                val sizeCol = cursor.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.SIZE)

                val albumArtistCol = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R) {
                    cursor.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.ALBUM_ARTIST)
                } else -1

                val genreCol = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R) {
                    cursor.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.GENRE)
                } else -1

                while (cursor.moveToNext()) {
                    val id = cursor.getLong(idCol)
                    val title = cursor.getString(titleCol) ?: "Sem título"
                    val artist = cursor.getString(artistCol) ?: "Artista desconhecido"
                    val album = cursor.getString(albumCol) ?: "Álbum desconhecido"
                    val durationMs = cursor.getLong(durCol)
                    val path = if (dataCol != -1) cursor.getString(dataCol) ?: "" else ""
                    val folder = if (path.isNotEmpty()) {
                        File(path).parentFile?.name ?: "Música"
                    } else {
                        "Música"
                    }

                    val year = if (yearCol != -1) cursor.getInt(yearCol) else 0
                    val rawTrack = if (trackCol != -1) cursor.getInt(trackCol) else 0
                    val trackNumber = if (rawTrack >= 1000) rawTrack % 1000 else rawTrack
                    val discNumber = if (rawTrack >= 1000) rawTrack / 1000 else 1
                    val composer = if (compCol != -1) cursor.getString(compCol) ?: "" else ""
                    val dateAdded = if (daCol != -1) cursor.getLong(daCol) else 0L
                    val dateModified = if (dmCol != -1) cursor.getLong(dmCol) else 0L
                    val size = if (sizeCol != -1) cursor.getLong(sizeCol) else 0L

                    val albumArtist = if (albumArtistCol != -1) cursor.getString(albumArtistCol) ?: "" else ""
                    val genre = if (genreCol != -1) cursor.getString(genreCol) ?: "" else ""

                    // Extension format tag
                    val ext = if (path.contains('.')) path.substringAfterLast('.').uppercase() else "AUDIO"
                    val formatTag = ext

                    // Merge stats
                    val stat = statsMap[id]
                    val playCount = stat?.playCount ?: 0
                    val lastPlayed = stat?.lastPlayedMs ?: 0L
                    val rating = stat?.rating ?: 0

                    val trackUri = ContentUris.withAppendedId(MediaStore.Audio.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI, id)

                    result.add(
                        Track(
                            id = id,
                            title = title,
                            artist = artist,
                            album = album,
                            durationMs = durationMs,
                            uri = trackUri,
                            path = path,
                            folder = folder,
                            albumArtist = albumArtist,
                            composer = composer,
                            genre = genre,
                            year = year,
                            trackNumber = trackNumber,
                            discNumber = discNumber,
                            fileSize = size,
                            dateAddedSec = dateAdded,
                            dateModifiedSec = dateModified,
                            playCount = playCount,
                            lastPlayedTimestamp = lastPlayed,
                            rating = rating,
                            formatTag = formatTag,
                            replayGainTrackGain = 0.0f,
                            replayGainTrackPeak = 1.0f,
                            replayGainAlbumGain = 0.0f,
                            replayGainAlbumPeak = 1.0f
                        )
                    )
                }
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}

        return result
    }
}

        if (raw.isBlank()) return emptyList()
        return raw.split(SEARCH_DELIMITER).map { it.trim() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
    }

    fun addRecentSearch(query: String) {
        val trimmed = query.trim()
        if (trimmed.isEmpty()) return
        val current = getRecentSearches().toMutableList()
        current.removeAll { it.equals(trimmed, ignoreCase = true) }
        current.add(0, trimmed)
        val trimmedList = current.take(8)
        prefs.edit().putString(KEY_RECENT_SEARCHES, trimmedList.joinToString(SEARCH_DELIMITER)).apply()
    }

    fun removeRecentSearch(query: String) {
        val trimmed = query.trim()
        val current = getRecentSearches().toMutableList()
        current.removeAll { it.equals(trimmed, ignoreCase = true) }
        prefs.edit().putString(KEY_RECENT_SEARCHES, current.joinToString(SEARCH_DELIMITER)).apply()
    }

    fun clearRecentSearches() {
        prefs.edit().remove(KEY_RECENT_SEARCHES).apply()
    }

    // --- Áudio Espacial (DSP Binaural) ---

    var spatialMode: Int

        set(value) = prefs.edit().putInt(KEY_SPATIAL_MODE, value).apply()
